The aim of this regulation is to determine the procedures and basis for promotion and title changes, based on service requirements and personnel planning, within the framework of merit and career principles. The rise in office is a concept in accordance with the Act of State Officers No. 657, which states the appointment of officers in the same or other service classes as the manager, branch manager, specialist, chef, and the special regulations of the institutions. The title change exam represents the examination to be assigned to titles that have been earned at least a secondary level of vocational or technical education. This is the test for staff, such as technicians, technicians, engineering. Those appointed by promotion or title change in office must succeed in writing and verbal examinations. The postings for the written exam will be announced before the exam, the application dates are set, and then, by reviewing the applications, they will be declared from the official websites of the institutions carrying the required conditions. Starting with the candidate who won the highest ranking in the written exam, the candidate will be placed on a verbal test to a certain extent. The arithmetic average of each member's scores is determined by the verbal test score of the staff. According to the specified list of success sequences, you will also be assigned to the empty staff. President's criminal responsibility MADDE 105- The President may be asked to be investigated by the majority of the Turkish Grand National Assembly's integer on charge of committing a crime. The Parliament may discuss the motion within a month at the latest and decide to open an investigation with the secret vote of three-thirds of the member integer. If it is decided to open an investigation, a 15-person commission of each political party will investigate among the candidates to whom the political parties in parliament will triple the number of members they can give to the commission in their power. The Commission will submit its report announcing the outcome of the investigation to the Parliamentary Presidency within two months. If the investigation is not completed during this time, the commission will be given a new and final month. The report will be distributed within ten days of its appointment as President, and it will be discussed in the General Assembly within ten days of its distribution. Turkey's Grand National Assembly can move to the Supreme Court with a secret vote of two-thirds of its integer. The Supreme Court's trial will be completed within three months, and if it cannot be completed in this period, then a three-month extension will be granted, and the trial will be final. The President, who has been ordered to be investigated, cannot be elected. The post of the President, convicted of a crime that prevents him from being elected in the Supreme Court, will be terminated. This clause shall be carried out after the president's completion of his duties for alleged crimes during his time in office. There are seven motions on the 9th article, five of which are the same property.

Vice presidents, proxy and ministers for the President MADDE 106-The President can appoint one or more Vice Presidents after he is elected. If the presidential office is discharged for any reason, the presidential election will be held within 45 days. Until the new one is elected, the vice president will take over the presidency and use the President's powers. If the general election is within a year or less, Turkey's election of the Grand National Assembly will be renewed with the election of the President. If the general election is more than a year away, the elected President, the Grand National Assembly of Turkey, will continue its mandate until election date. For the President who completes the remaining time, that time is not of the essence. Both elections are held together at the time of the Grand National Assembly of Turkey's general elections. In the event that the president temporarily leaves his post for reasons such as sickness and leave the country, the vice president will be acting as acting president and using the President's authority. Vice presidents and ministers are appointed and dismissed by the President among those qualified to be elected MP. Vice Presidents and ministers, written in Article 81, swear in front of the Turkish Grand National Assembly. If they are appointed as members of the Grand National Assembly of Turkey, vice president or minister, their membership will end. Vice presidents and ministers are responsible to the President. On charges of crimes against the vice presidents and ministers, an investigation could be launched by the ruling majority of the Turkish Grand National Assembly's integer. The Parliament may discuss the motion within a month at the latest and decide to open an investigation with the secret vote of three-thirds of the member integer. If it is decided to open an investigation, a 15-person commission will be held by parliament's political parties to name each political party as three times the number of members they will be able to give to the commission as much as their power. The Commission will submit its report announcing the outcome of the investigation to the Parliamentary Presidency within two months. If the investigation is not completed during this time, the commission will be given a new and final month. The report will be distributed within ten days of its appointment and will be discussed in the General Assembly within ten days of its distribution. Turkey's Grand National Assembly can move to the Supreme Court with a secret vote of two-thirds of its integer. The Supreme Court's trial will be completed within three months, and if it cannot be completed in this period, then a three-month extension will be granted, and the trial will be final. During the time they are in office, sentences of fifth, sixth, and seventh are carried out in terms of alleged crimes involving their duties. The position of the vice president or minister convicted of a crime that prevents him from being elected in the Supreme Court will be terminated. Vice presidents and ministers benefit from provisions concerning the immunity of law in crimes that are not relevant to their duties. The establishment, removal of the ministries, duties and powers of the ministry and the establishment of central and rural organisations are organised by the presidential decree.
